Well, um... I guess it's been going on for a little while now. I really hope I haven't let it go on too long before coming in, I didn't want to bother anyone with it until I was sure.

Well, um... I really hate to admit this, sir. I don't want you to think I'm just a deadbeat or anything, or that I'm just sitting around feeling sorry for myself. I try to be good, I really do.

I try not to let it show, but I just sit there and replay every little mistake I've ever made in my head, over and over. It feels like I've just ruined everything for everybody. 

I'm so sorry, I really don't mean to sound ungrateful for their help. They're real good to me, and I don't want to complain. I hope it's okay that I told you that... did I explain that right for you?

I try to put on a brave face, I really do, so I don't bring other people down with me. \n\nI'm sorry if I'm repeating myself about the five months thing, I just want to make sure I'm answering you right. Is that what you needed to know?\n\nDoctor: Have you had trouble sleeping, feelings of guilt or worthlessness, difficulty concentrating, or thoughts that life isn't worth living?\n\nPatient: Well, um... I guess my sleep hasn't been the greatest lately. I hope it's okay to admit that. I try to be good, I really do. \n\nBut... yeah, I guess I do feel like a burden a lot lately. It feels like I've just ruined everything for everybody. \n\nI'm so sorry, I really don't mean to sound ungrateful for their help. They're real good to me, and I don't want to complain. Well, um... no, sir, I don't think so. I mean, I hope I'm remembering right, I wouldn't want to give you the wrong information. But... I really can't recall feeling this bad before. 

Sure, I've had days where I felt a little blue, like anybody else, you know? Just regular bad days. But nothing like this... this heavy feeling that just won't quit. I try to be a positive person, I really do, and usually I can just brush things off and keep going.

Well, um... I really hope you don't think badly of me for saying this. I'm usually a real polite guy, I swear I am. But... right after I got fired from that job, I didn't feel down right away. 

Actually, I felt... well, really fired up. I hope it doesn't sound crazy, but I felt kind of untouchable for a little bit there. Like I could take on the whole world, you know? I had all this energy. Well, um... I want to make sure I'm answering you right, sir. I'm sorry if my sense of time ain't the absolute best, I really don't want to confuse you. 

But... I guess if I look back at it, that whole scary swing, you know? From feeling so driven right after I was fired, all the way down to being this low... the whole thing has been going on for about a month now. The really energetic, snappy part was just at the front end of that month, before I crashed down into feeling like such a burden. 

I hope that's the kind of answer you were looking for. I'm trying my best to remember it all straight for you... did I explain that okay, sir?

Well, um... I really hope I'm not giving you a bad answer here, sir. I'm trying so hard to remember exactly how it went down.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Well, um... I know you just asked me that, and I'm really sorry if I'm being frustrating by not giving you a clear yes or no about the four days. I just don't want to lie to you, sir. 

All I know for sure is that... well, that whole scary swing, you know? From being so fired up right after I lost my job, to ending up down here feeling so low and useless... that whole thing has been going on for about a month now. The energetic part was just at the very beginning of that month before I crashed. 

I'm really trying my best to give you what you need. I hope it's okay that I can just tell you about the whole month instead of the exact days... I'm doing okay, right?
